Talents Training
Tianli International Holdings has always regarded young teachers as the core force for promoting educational
innovation and improving teaching quality, and attaches great importance to their cultivation and development.
During the reporting year, the Company continued to deepen, implement and promote the mentor-apprentice
twinning program of “Up-and-coming Elites Project”. Being established between senior teachers and energetic
young teachers with great development potential, the close mentoring relationship focuses on improving the
moral cultivation, professional skills, and scientific research level of young teachers, thereby effectively realizing
the intergenerational inheritance of educational wisdom and practical experience. Luzhou Tianli School has
established 50 pairs of mentors and apprentices, integrating the “Up-and-coming Elites Project” into daily
teaching scenarios, and promoting the direct application of high-quality experience to the classroom through the
practical effect of “imparting, helping and leading”, helping young teachers grow rapidly.

Requirements of the “Up-and-coming Elites Project”:

(1)    High standards for selecting mentors: Mentors shall perform their duties well to the implementation of
       “imparting, helping and leading”. Mentors are required to make their teaching plans and classes public so
       that young teachers can attend and learn in a timely manner.

(2)    Delineate the scope and personnel of young teachers and voluntarily select mentors: In principle, each
       mentor shall instruct no more than two young teachers. Young teachers are required to write detailed
       plans, prepare lessons by class time, and seek advice from mentors on teaching plans.

(3)    Develop a twinning class attendances schedule designated for the “Up-and-coming Elites Project”: Young
       teachers are required to attend at least 2 classes by the mentor every week, and the mentor is required
       to attend no less than 8 classes by young teachers per semester, which shall cover preparing lessons,
       attending lessons, demonstrating lessons, and evaluating lessons.

(4)    Develop a corresponding assessment and reward system: The Teacher Development Center shall keep
       track of the inspection records, taking them as an important basis for the final assessment of young
       teachers and mentors.

SUSTAINABLE DEVELOPMENT
Ensuring Operation Compliance
Tianli International Holdings regards compliance management as pivotal to the stable development of the
enterprise. As an enterprise shouldering the mission of educational operation and comprehensive services,
Tianli International Holdings has always abided by national laws and regulations and regulatory provisions
such as the Listing Rules of the Hong Kong Stock Exchange, and has established and continuously improved a
compliance management system and institutional framework. The Company closely follows changes in policies
and regulations, and promptly identifies and prevents compliance risks through comprehensive supervision and
inspection, in-depth compliance review, special audits, and a strict accountability mechanism, providing strong
support for the Company’s sustained high-quality development.

Risk Control
Tianli International Holdings strictly complies with laws and regulations such as the Company Law of the People’s
Republic of China and the Basic Standards for Enterprise Internal Control, and continuously revises and improves
internal management policies such as the “Risk Control System” and the “Brand Promotion Management
System”. The Company has established a risk control and internal audit center system jointly composed of the
Audit and Supervision Department, the Internal Control Department, and the Legal Department, and has fully
integrated risk management into daily operations, continuously improving the risk management capabilities at all
levels.

The Company attaches great importance to risk prevention and control, and conducts a comprehensive annual
internal control inspection to conduct a horizontal comparative analysis of various types of risks, and proposes
major risk management measures and improvement strategies. During the reporting period, the Internal Control
Department under the Risk Control and Internal Audit Center completed a number of audit tasks, including
internal control audits, engineering audits, and bidding audits. Through the systematic implementation of internal
audits, the Company has sorted out and optimized the systems and processes with deficiencies, formulated
and implemented rectification plans, continuously enhanced the risk prevention awareness of all employees,
comprehensively improved the overall risk control capabilities, and consolidated the foundation of compliant
operations.

The Company has fully integrated compliance awareness into its corporate culture construction, requiring
employees of each project to conduct monthly summaries and reflections on risk issues. The Company also
organizes special training via external online platforms to continuously strengthen employees’ risk prevention
and control awareness. During the reporting period, the Company conducted special training covering compliant
operations, anti-corruption, and integrity for the management team and business backbones. The trainees
included senior management, middle-level executives, and personnel in key positions of the Company and
its schools, totalling approximately 360 attendees. The training was conducted in a variety of forms, such as
knowledge competitions and case studies, to ensure that employees fully understand the concepts and tools of
risk management, and to enhance the Company’s ability to operate in a standardized manner.

Tianli International Holdings attaches great importance to the construction of anti-corruption and integrity, upholds

Responsible Procurement
To practice the concept of sustainable procurement, Tianli International Holdings strictly adheres to the
Management System for Procurement and Bidding, and conducts strict screening across multiple dimensions
such as environmental protection, labor rights protection, and business ethics compliance, and gives priority
to partners demonstrating outstanding performance in sustainable development. By signing a responsible
procurement agreement, the obligations of both parties in terms of environmental and social responsibility are
clarified, and suppliers are actively guided to promote green production methods to reduce resource consumption
and environmental pollution.

Audit and Evaluation
The Company has established a complete supplier audit system to implement differentiated management of the
entire process of supplier admission, performance evaluation, and exit. This system sets seven core dimensions:
company qualifications, financial health, warehousing and distribution capabilities, cooperation potential, strategic
synergy, execution capabilities, and operating conditions, and rates suppliers into five levels from AA to C and a
non-compliant blacklist. In daily management, the Company conducts dynamic evaluation of each batch of raw
materials covering product quality and service management, and initiates differentiated disposal mechanisms
for non-compliant suppliers based on the rating results, including special approval applications, time-limited
rectification, or direct disqualification, to achieve closed-loop optimization and continuous improvement of the
supply chain.

Advocating Green Operations
Tianli International Holdings regards environmental protection as its core corporate responsibility and actively
builds a green, ecological, and harmonious campus environment. The Company abides by the Environmental
Protection Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Law on the Prevention and Control of Environmental
Pollution of Solid Waste of the People’s Republic of China and other laws and regulations. In campus
construction, priority is given to the use of environmentally friendly building materials, scientific planning of
green landscapes, and continuous improvement of campus ecological diversity. In education and teaching,
environmental education courses are systematically carried out, low-carbon living is advocated, and students
are encouraged to participate in energy conservation and emission reduction practices, effectively enhancing the
environmental awareness of teachers and students, and contributing to the green and sustainable development
of society through practical actions.

Green Campus
Tianli International Holdings continues to improve the environmental management system of each campus, and
revises and implements internal management systems such as the Green School Office System. The Company
improves resource utilization efficiency through measures such as optimizing the energy structure, promoting
the application of energy-saving equipment, and strengthening the whole-process management of waste. At the
same time, it actively advocates green commuting and paperless office, and guides teachers, students, and staff
to practice low-carbon behaviors, and comprehensively builds a green campus ecosystem from infrastructure to
daily operations.

Saving Water and Electricity
Special plans for energy conservation and emission reduction are formulated and implemented in each campus,
and the start and stop schedule of the lighting systems in the teaching and living areas are scientifically set to
effectively avoid long-running lights and idle equipment. The day-to-day maintenance and data-based monitoring
of water supply facilities are strengthened, a daily statistics and comparison mechanism for water and electricity
consumption is established, and metering devices are regularly calibrated. Abnormal water consumption is
promptly traced to its source to prevent the wastage of resources caused by long-term leakage. A system of
daily recording, weekly summary, and monthly evaluation of energy consumption data is implemented to build an
energy-saving management system with full participation and full traceability.

Green Commuting
In compliance with the “safe, economical, and standardized” principle, the unified dispatch of official vehicles is
implemented, and special accounting is carried out for fuel consumption and tolls that exceed the normal range.
Through quantitative management, unreasonable use of vehicles is effectively restrained, reducing greenhouse
gas emissions caused by excessive use of vehicles.

Green Office
The refined management of electricity consumption during working hours is fully implemented, and the lighting,
air conditioning, and ventilation equipment in meeting places are required to be turned off in a timely manner.
Paperless office is actively advocated, by circulating the electronic documents to reduce the printing of paper
documents. Special recycling facilities are set up to classify and collect office paper, newspapers, and packaging
materials. The use of environmentally friendly office supplies such as refillable writing tools, recycled toner
cartridges, and rechargeable batteries is promoted to comprehensively improve the efficiency of resource
recycling.

Environmental Improvement
The campus greening and beautification project is systematically promoted, with the maintenance and
management of flowers and trees strengthened, and the campus green coverage rate continuously increased.
Attention is paid to the organic combination of campus landscape and humanistic education, creating an
ecological environment with rich cultural connotations, achieving the educational effect of “integrating teaching
into the scenery and integrating the scenery with teaching”, and playing the dual role of environmental
beautification and humanistic quality cultivation.

Hazardous Waste Treatment
To standardize the waste management on campus, the Human Resources and Administration Department has
established a special ledger for hazardous waste, which records in detail the source, specific type, quantity,
flow, and disposal method of various types of hazardous waste. Each campus implements a system of
designated storage and dedicated personnel management, and regularly entrusts third-party organizations with
corresponding qualifications for compliant recycling and harmless treatment.

Non-hazardous Waste Treatment
For general waste, various departments actively implement reduction measures, including promoting double-sided
printing to reduce paper consumption, giving priority to the use of recyclable office supplies, and advocating that
employees bring their own water cups to reduce the use of disposable paper cups. At the same time, special
recycling facilities are set up in the office area to classify and collect recyclable waste such as waste paper and
newspapers, which are subsequently processed for resource recovery by designated recycling units.


 Case: Cangxi Tianli School Launches Energy Conservation and Emission Reduction Activities

 In 2025, to deeply implement the energy conservation and emission reduction goal and jointly create a green
 and low-carbon campus, Cangxi Tianli School actively promoted a number of energy-saving management
 measures. The school issued the Campus Energy Conservation and Emission Reduction Initiative, advocating
 green behaviors such as saving electricity and water to all teachers and students. At the same time, it
 intensified daily reminders and supervision of energy conservation and consumption reduction in each class,
 and regularly announced the inspection results to promote the normalization and institutionalization of energy
 conservation and emission reduction work.




                                                                                                                  203
Tianli International Holdings Limited
Annual Report 2025




Green Education
Tianli International Holdings integrates the concept of green education throughout the entire process of
teaching practice, and focuses on cultivating students’ environmental awareness and the concept of sustainable
development. The school systematically introduces environmental science content in its curriculum and organizes
extracurricular practical activities with distinct themes to guide students to deepen their understanding of
environmental protection concepts through participation and consciously practice a green lifestyle. At the same
time, the Company actively collaborates with the community to carry out environmental protection and public
welfare projects, and promotes the impact of green education from the campus to a wider social level. Through
multi-dimensional and systematic educational practices, Tianli International Holdings is committed to cultivating
green citizens with a sense of environmental responsibility and practical ability, and continues to assist in the
promotion and deepening of the sustainability concept among the new generation.

Green Teaching System
The Company advocates the deep integration of environmental education into the school’s conventional
system, and establishes a school environmental education division of labor and responsibility system led by the
principal’s office. According to the functions of each department, annual environmental education work goals are
set separately, and teachers are encouraged to integrate environmental protection concepts into the teaching
process in combination with the characteristics of their subjects.

Curriculum System Reform
Each campus clearly lists environmental education as a teaching task every semester, which is fully reflected in
the selection of textbooks and teaching plans, achieving an organic combination of curriculum learning and the
cultivation of environmental awareness, and guiding students to establish environmental thinking and form green
habits in a subtle way.

Development of Diversified Environmental Protection Activities
The academic affair office of the school and the head teachers of each class collaborate to incorporate various
forms of environmental protection themed activities into the focus of students’ moral education. Using important
festivals and anniversaries, extracurricular team building activities such as speech contests, social practices,
and volunteer services are organized, supplemented by green publicity and education and regular environmental
lectures, and a campus culture that emphasizes ecological civilization is built through multiple measures.

Responding to Climate Change
Tianli International Holdings actively responds to the national goals of carbon peaking and carbon neutrality, and
fully integrates the low-carbon concept into campus operations and educational practices. In terms of campus
construction, the Company continuously reduces the consumption of fossil fuels by optimizing building energy
efficiency design and strengthening energy system management. In the curriculum system, teaching content
related to climate change and sustainable development is added to enhance students’ low-carbon awareness
and cultivate future talents to cope with climate change. At the same time, the school actively promotes garbage
classification and resource utilization, encourages teachers and students to participate in energy conservation
and emission reduction practices, effectively reduces greenhouse gas emissions during waste disposal, and is
committed to creating a green and low-carbon campus demonstration, making positive contributions to achieving
the “dual carbon” goals.

enhance climate resilience and strategic foresight.

SCHOOL-COMMUNITY COOPERATION
The growth of talents relies not only on the careful nurturing provided by schools, but also requires the forging
of its value through practical engagement with society. Tianli International Holdings aggregates family and social
resources with the concept of collaborative education, focusing on creating a talent cultivation hub that meets
societal demands, and empowers students to enhance their comprehensive competitiveness for the future. At the
same time, the Company has always adhered to its mission as a corporate citizen, giving back to society with a
public welfare spirit, and continuously conveying warmth and positive energy to the community through regular
public welfare practices.

Families and Schools Join Together
Tianli International Holdings continues to promote home-school collaboration, innovate ways of home-school
co-education, build efficient communication channels, and promote close cooperation between family education
and school education. Teachers in each school establish a stable home-school cooperation network through daily
communication tools (such as class WeChat groups) and regular parent meetings, which not only ensures timely
communication of information, but also improves teaching effectiveness through the joint efforts of home and
school, helping students to develop in an all-round way.

In terms of stratified and classified guidance, the Company adopts differentiated home-school co-education
strategies tailored to the characteristics of different educational stages. For the kindergarten stage, parent-child
activities such as fun sports games and Arbor Day practice are organized to foster an emotional communication
platform while strengthening home-school communication. For the high school stage, the focus is on monitoring
students’ learning progress and psychological wellbeing to ensure that home-school collaboration achieves
maximum effectiveness.


 Case: Rizhao Tianli School Launched a Series of Home-School Collaborative Education Activities

 In 2025, with home-school co-education as the core, Rizhao Tianli School established a high-frequency
 interaction mechanism and condensed educational consensus by regularly carrying out activities such as
 home-school forums, parent open days, home-school talks, and all-member home visits. At the same time,
 initiatives including canteen experience sessions, cafeteria tours, and participation in labor education classes
 enabled parents to gain first-hand insight into the school’s distinctive approaches to catering management and
 labor education, facilitating deeper involvement in the students’ developmental journey. This suite of initiatives
 not only strengthened the bond of trust between home and school, but also conveyed the Company’s “student-
 oriented” values through transparent and participatory engagement.




                                                                                                                   207
Tianli International Holdings Limited
Annual Report 2025




Enthusiastic in Public Welfare
Tianli International Holdings has always taken rooting in society and giving back to society as its own
responsibility, and actively advocates and promotes all employees to participate in public welfare undertakings,
and encourages employees to spontaneously organize and deeply engage in educational public welfare activities.

In 2025, the Company continued to deepen its layout in public welfare undertakings, focusing on key areas such
as donation and student aid, and poverty alleviation, conveying corporate warmth with practical actions.

In the future, we will remain steadfast in our original intention of public welfare, and actively explore new
pathways for social forces to participate in public welfare through model innovation and resource integration.
We look forward to empowering the development of education with more targeted public welfare practices, and
mobilizing the forces of all sectors of society to collectively foster a mutually supportive and progressive social
environment, so as to pave the way for broader opportunities in the growth of the next generation.


 Case: Lanzhou Tianli School’s Paired Assistance Initiative for Rural Schools in Yuzhong County

 In 2025, adhering to the original intention of educational balance and rural revitalization, Lanzhou Tianli School
 established a paired assistance relationship with two rural schools in Yuzhong County, Gansu Province,
 delivering high-quality resources and care to local teachers and students. We dispatched 12 core teachers
 to conduct 24 outreach teaching sessions covering the full spectrum of primary to high school curricula,
 introducing advanced teaching methodologies and curriculum frameworks to rural classrooms. At the same
 time, we donated 12,000 books and more than 300 teaching instruments, while organizing on-the-job training
 for rural teachers at our campus, with over 60 participants sharing access to specialized curriculum resources
 such as the Lida Thinking Reading 3.0 program. Such assistance initiative not only improved the educational
 facilities of the recipient schools, but also promoted the sharing of educational resources between urban and
 rural areas, embodying the Group’s social responsibility and educational mission.
